      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "The world of TerraFirmaCraft is divided into large continents - landmasses many kilometers wide and separated by oceans. In these, you may find mountain ranges, rivers, and many other environments.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "A typical TFC world viewed at a large scale.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "The world is also divided up into different types of Rock. Rock regions can be over a kilometer across, and there will usually be two or three different rock layers under your feet at all times. As different ores are found in different rock types, locating specific rock types can be very important for finding resources such as Ores, which will often only appear in certain rock types.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Rock Layers",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "The cross-section of a TFC world.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Ocean floors are composed of igneous extrusive rock - rocks that are formed by magma which cools quickly. Underneath igneous extrusive rock will likely be a igneous intrusive rock of the same grade. For example, under Basalt (a mafic, igneous extrusive rock), will likely be Gabbro (a mafic, igneous intrusive rock).",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Ocean Floors",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "- Felsic rocks are Granite and Rhyolite. - Intermediate rocks are Andesite, Dacite, and Diorite. - Mafic rocks are Basalt and Gabbro.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Igneous Rock Grades",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "The top layer of rock on a continent will either be igneous extrusive, or sedimentary. Underneath sedimentary rocks will likely be metamorphosed forms of the rock above. For example, Marble (a metamorphic rock) will likely be found under Limestone or Chalk. High grade metamorphic rocks are found deep under other metamorphic or igneous rocks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "- Slate forms under Shale, Claystone, and Conglomerate. - Marble forms under Limestone, Dolomite, and Chalk. - Quartzite forms under Chert. - Phyllite forms under Slate - Schist and Gneiss form under Phyllite, or other igneous intrusive rocks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Metamorphic Rocks",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Finally, in mountainous regions you might also see uplift, where a metamorphic or igneous intrusive rock is found on the surface. Uplift rocks can be found above other continental sedimentary or higher-grade metamorphic rocks. In addition, Dikes - small vertical slices of igneous intrusive rock - may appear scattered around the world,",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Uplift Regions",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "protruding through the upper layers of rocks. With all that in mind, the next few pages list the rocks of all four categories: Sedimentary, Metamorphic, Igneous Extrusive, and Igneous Intrusive. These categories determine where the rock can spawn (see the previous pages), and also what ores may spawn in this rock.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Sedimentary rocks are formed by the accumulation or deposition of mineral or organic particles. They are typically found on the top layers of rock in continental areas. They are: - Shale - Claystone - Limestone - Conglomerate - Dolomite - Chert - Chalk",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Metamorphic rocks are created by a process called metamorphism. They can be found underneath corresponding sedimentary or igneous rock, or in uplift areas. They are: - Quartzite - Slate - Phyllite - Schist - Gneiss - Marble",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Igneous Extrusive rocks are formed from magma cooling on the Earth's surface. They can be found on the top layer of rock in continental areas, or on the floor of oceans. They are: - Rhyolite - Basalt - Andesite - Dacite",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Igneous Extrusive",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Igneous Intrusive rocks are formed from magma which cooled under the Earth's crust. They can be found deep underground, or rarely in dikes or uplift areas. They are: - Granite - Diorite - Gabbro",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/geology.json",
          "text": "Igneous Intrusive",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.title",
          "label": "Geology",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Building Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "The building gadget makes building a large number of blocks all at once extremely easy! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to build with.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Building Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Build to Me. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to place the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Build Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be built",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, building will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Don't forget you can undo if you make a mistake!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "The copy paste gadget makes replicating structures far easier! To start, ensure you're in 'copy' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a green outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {

        {

          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "To see a list of the items you need, open the settings menu and choose the 'material list' option. This is a scrollable list showing what items you do or do not have, and can be sorted in multiple ways. Try it out!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",

          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Needed items",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Its possible to make fine tuned adjustments to the copy area by opening the Adjust Placement window in the settings menu. From here, you can adjust the position of the start block and end block. Click the plus and minus buttons to see the green box change in real time. You can also swap to 'absolute' coordinates mode and enter in-game world coordinates instead.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Adjusting Copy",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the area you are copying",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Theres a similar option for paste mode. In this UI, you'll be adjusting the location of the starting block. Click the buttons and the render should update in-world in real time.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Adjusting Paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the location of the paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The cut paste gadget makes moving structures a breeze, and works just like the Copy Paste Gadget! To start, ensure you're in 'cut' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a red outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cutting a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The structure will not be removed until you click the 'cut' button in the settings menu. Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. To place the structure in world, simply right click! No items are necessary for this, as its using the blocks you removed earlier.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "You can only have one structure stored on the gadget at a time. Placing it in the world will erased the stored information on the gadget. Be careful when pasting the structure, you only get one chance, exchange mode is recommended! If you attempt to cut a new area while data already exists, you'll be prompted to confirm. If you do this, the first set of data will be lost forever!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "All block-entity data will be moved along with the structure, including the contents of chests and block configurations. Much like the Copy Paste Gadget, you can modify the cut or paste positions with the appropriate settings menu option. See that chapter for details!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "The destruction gadget makes clearing a large area very easy. Any blocks cleared will be voided!. First, open the settings menu and adjust the area. This area is with respect to the block (and block face) you are looking at. If you look at the top face of a block, 'up' will be relative to the player's screen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destroying an area, click the arrow to see diffent pictures!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Once you're satisfied with the settings, right click on a block to void all the highlighted blocks. They will be removed from the world. You may undo this action if you make a mistake. It will not remove items from your inventory to undo, as the gadget is just returning the voided blocks to the world.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "The exchanging gadget can swap one set of blocks for another! It cannot, however, replace air blocks. Use a Building Gadget for that! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to exchange to.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Exchanging Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Surface. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed over top of existing blocks. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to exchange the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchange Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be exchanged",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, exchanging will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Items for the removed blocks will be returned to you. Note: You can enchant this tool with silk touch. Without it grass will return dirt, etc.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "You can undo with the exchanging gadget, but you must have the necessary items. For example, if you exchanged grass into stone bricks, you must have grass in your inventory to undo this exchange. Don't forget to apply silk touch if desired!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor.json",
          "text": "Anchor allows you to 'lock in' the block you're looking at, so you can freely move or look around. There is a hotkey for this, or you can access it from the settings menu. You can right click at any time to build the structure when it is anchored. Note: Changing range may not take effect until you remove and replace the anchor.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Anchor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor.json",
          "text": "Anchor your render!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Anchor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        }
      ],
      "aliases": []
    },
    {
      "id": "tfc:primitive/anvils",
      "label": "Primitive Anvils",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils",

        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils.json",
          "text": "Primitive Anvils",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Primitive Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils.json",
          "text": "An alternative to casting tools directly in the early game, and a requirement for higher tier metals, is to use an Anvil. An anvil is a block which can be used for two different processes: working and welding. An anvil can weld double ingots of the metal type needed to create the next tier anvil above it, which will be necessary to work other items from that metal.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Primitive Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils.json",
          "text": "First, you need to acquire a block of raw rock, that is igneous extrusive (Rhyolite, Basalt, Andesite, or Dacite) or igneous intrusive (Granite, Diorite, or Gabbro). You could find and use an exposed block in the world, or you could extract one from the surrounding rock.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Primitive Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils.json",
          "text": "You will also need any material of hammer. In order to make the anvil, simply right click the exposed top face of one of those raw rock blocks with your hammer, and voila! An anvil will be formed. Anvils have tiers and the rock anvil is tier 0 - the lowest tier. It is only able to weld tier I ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Primitive Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils.json",
          "text": "Rock Anvil",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Primitive Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils.json",
          "text": "Converting the center raw rock to an anvil.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Primitive Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils.json",
          "text": "In order to obtain a raw rock block without breaking it into smaller rocks, it needs to be extracted. You must mine the blocks on all six sides of a raw rock block - once it is surrounded by air on all sides - it will pop off as a item which can be picked up.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Primitive Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils.json",
          "text": "Obtaining Raw Rock",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Primitive Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils.json",
          "text": "Mining all six sides of a piece of raw stone - once complete, the center block will pop off as an item.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Primitive Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils.json",
          "text": "Welding is a process through which two items are fused together to create a new item. Welding works the same whether on a stone anvil or a metal anvil. First, you must place the two items you want to weld on the anvil. You can do this either by using the items on the anvil, or by opening the anvil interface and inserting them in the two leftmost slots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Primitive Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_anvils.json",
          "text": "You also need to have at least one flux in the anvil to aid the welding process. Then, while both items are hot enough to weld - the tooltip will say \"Can Weld\" - you must use any hammer on the anvil or press the weld button on the anvil interface. You will hear a hammering sound and the items will be welded together.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Primitive Anvils",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Better Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Freezer",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel is hard to get; it requires venturing out to the ocean, looking for oil spouts. The Cryo Freezer introduces a new way to obtain a better fuel - Cryo Fuel. Cryo Fuel is much more efficient than refined fuel, allowing you to launch a rocket with just one bucket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "It's created by collecting ice shards, which are found as an ore underground on cold planets. The ice shard then needs to be inserted into a Cryo Freezer, and it will convert to 25 mB of Cryo Fuel. Alternatively, you can convert ice, packed ice and blue ice to fuel. Once you've made at least two buckets (one for a launch and the other for a return trip), place the buckets in the rocket fuel slot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "In addition to being an excellent fuel, cryo fuel has some other features. When placed, it will damage anything inside, similar to lava. It also freezes water, allowing for an infinite ice source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/venus_and_mercury",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/venus_and_mercury.json",
          "text": "Venus and Mercury",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Venus and Mercury",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/venus_and_mercury",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/venus_and_mercury.json",
          "text": "Once you have enough Ostrum to craft a complete netherite space suit set and all of the components of the tier three rocket, you can travel to Venus and Mercury. Please note that if you enter these planets without a netherite space suit or fire resistance, you will die very fast. The next material that you seek is Calorite.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Venus and Mercury",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/venus_and_mercury",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/venus_and_mercury.json",
          "text": "Calorite is used to craft the ultimate rocket capable of travelling to a new solar system.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Venus and Mercury",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Even after you place your rocket, you will not be able to fly just yet. To fly, you will need to fill the rocket with fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Obtaining Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Oil is found in large amounts spouting over the ocean surface. Collect as much oil as you can, as oil is non-renewable, and you need a lot of it for each rocket launch.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Obtaining Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "After collecting enough oil (at least six buckets for a launch and return), you will need to refine the oil into fuel. To do this, you will need to craft a fuel refinery. Power it, then place the oil in the left slot, and it will be refined into fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel Refinery",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Shift-click on the rocket to open its inventory, then place three buckets of fuel into the input slot. Place the other three buckets into the rocket's inventory for a return trip.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "At this point, you can launch your rocket; however, you will likely die very fast, as there is no oxygen on the moon. In order to survive on the moon, you will need an oxygenated space suit and an ample oxygen supply.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "To obtain oxygen, it must be extracted from water. An oxygen loader is required to do this. Craft one, then place water in the left tank, and watch it convert into oxygen. You must repeat this process many times until you have enough oxygen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Loader",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/oxygen.json",
          "text": "Oxygen can then be extracted by collecting it in a bucket, spacesuit, or a gas tank. After you have enough oxygen, you are moments away from launching your first rocket!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Oxygen",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/preparing_to_launch",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/preparing_to_launch.json",
          "text": "Preparing to Launch",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Preparing to Launch",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/preparing_to_launch",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/preparing_to_launch.json",
          "text": "Follow these steps very carefully! Missing even one thing here could make your life tough, or worse, cause you to be stranded on the moon! In order to return to earth from the moon, you need two basic things: a launch pad and three buckets of fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Preparing to Launch",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/preparing_to_launch",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/preparing_to_launch.json",
          "text": "Place these items in the inventory of your rocket, and ensure that you always have them every time you launch a rocket. Finally, you should bring multiple full gas tanks, plentiful food, soul torches or glowstone (normal torches will burn out), weapons and tools. Once you have this, you are ready to launch! Enter the rocket and press !",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Preparing to Launch",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "The Moon",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "The Moon",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "Congratulations on reaching the moon! The moon is a barren wasteland, grey and dead, as far as the eye can see; however, underground is where you will find the next major resource in Ad Astra - Desh! Desh is used to craft advanced machines, space stations, and the tier two rocket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "The environment",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "The environment of the moon is very different from earth. As the moon has no oxygen and is freezing, attempting to place water will freeze it and lit blocks like torches will break. Any plant life and crops will also die very quickly. To combat these effects, you can build an oxygen distributor, which will we cover later.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "Returning Home",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
          "text": "To return back to earth, open your lander (shift + right-click) and collect the rocket, fuel and the launch pad. To remove the lander, punch it. Set up the launch pad, place the rocket down and fill it up with fuel. Press and fly back to earth.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "The Moon",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_hot_planets/jet_suit",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_hot_planets/jet_suit.json",
          "text": "Jet Suit",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Jet Suit",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_hot_planets/jet_suit",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_hot_planets/jet_suit.json",
          "text": "Jet Suit",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Jet Suit",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_hot_planets/jet_suit",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_hot_planets/jet_suit.json",
          "text": "The Jet Suit is an upgraded version of the Netherite Space Suit, with more protection, additional oxygen storage, and flight. It's expensive to craft, but it offers high-speed flight, making travel easy.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Jet Suit",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_hot_planets/jet_suit",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_hot_planets/jet_suit.json",
          "text": "It's got two modes of flight: ascending () and boosted ( + ). Ascending makes the wearer fly upwards, while boosted is similar to an elytra that's constantly being propelled by fireworks. The Jet Suit is powered by energy and must be charged to fly. It can be charged with an energizer by right-clicking it with the jet suit. Enable/disable the suit by pressing ().",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Jet Suit",
          "item_ref_count": 6,

        {

          "text": "Once you have found enough Calorite to craft a tier four rocket, you will be able to leave this solar system and enter a new one. The first planet that you will head to in Proxima Centauri is Glacio. Glacio is brutally cold; however, it has oxygen, allowing you to survive there without a spacesuit.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Proxima Centauri",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_hot_planets/proxima_centauri",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_hot_planets/proxima_centauri.json",
          "text": "This is the end of the Ad Astra progression for now. This guidebook will be expanded as more content is added. Thanks for playing Ad Astra!",

          "label": "Proxima Centauri",

          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/cables",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/cables.json",
          "text": "Cables allow for long-range energy transfer. There are two variants: steel and desh – desh, having a higher transfer rate. When used with a wrench, cables can be configured to extend in directions or to disconnect from nodes in the network.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Cables",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/cables",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/cables.json",
          "text": "Cable ducts",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Cables",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/cables",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/cables.json",
          "text": "Cables are not solid blocks. As such, they will not seal a structure which prevents oxygen distributors and gravity normalizers from functioning. The cable duct is a full block that works exactly like cables while preventing leaks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Cables",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Throughout the moon, There are large, inhabited underground villages. These villages contain an alien form of the earth villager, who call themselves Lunarians. Unlike their earth counterpart, Lunarian don't need oxygen and can survive the chilling environment of the moon.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Lunarian have different trades than earth villagers, featuring planet-specific materials, space suits and oxygen gear.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Wandering Trader",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Lunarian Wandering Traders appear on every moon and planet. They have many rocket-related trades that may save your life, including launch pads, oxygen, fuel, soul torches and water. They accept emeralds, which are commonly found in the various structures on the moon and planets.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Wandering Trader",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.name",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/mars",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/mars.json",
          "text": "Now that you have explored the moon and worked with desh, you want to take it further and go to the red planet. Building a tier two rocket capable of travelling to Mars is similar to building the tier one rocket, but with desh materials instead of steel. Construct the parts, build the rocket in the NASA workbench, fuel it up, and off you go to Mars!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Mars",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/mars",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/mars.json",
          "text": "Ostrum is the next material that you seek on the red planet. It is used to craft more powerful space armour, build exceptional machines and to construct a rocket capable of withstanding extreme heat, allowing you to travel to hot planets.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Mars",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "The oxygen distributor is a powerful machine that distributes oxygen in a sealed room, allowing any life inside to survive. It works exactly like an oxygen loader, where you insert water into the tank, but instead of giving you oxygen, the oxygen is distributed into the air.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "Water Pump",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/oxygen_distributor.json",
          "text": "The water pump is essential for keeping the oxygen distributor running infinitely. To use it, place an infinite water source below, then place the water pump above one of the water blocks, and then place the oxygen distributor adjacent to it. Make sure to supply both the pump and the oxygen distributor with sufficient power.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Oxygen Distributor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 7
        }
